  \cstep{\D}{m}{\rho_1 \semi \rho_2}{\D'}{m'}{\rho_1' \semi \rho_2}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item In the former subcase, by \thref{thm:canonrt} (canonical forms),
we get $\rho_1 = ()$. Therefore, we can apply rule \ts{ER-Seq} to step
$\cstep{\D}{m}{() \semi \rho}{\D}{m}{\rho}$.
\item Thus, in either case we can step, and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Prog}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Prog] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\tprog{\Gamma}{P}{\Omega'}}
  \and
  \Delta[\C_0] = \pair{\tone}{\emptyset}
  \and
  \dom(\Delta) = \Omega \cup \Omega' \cup \{\C_0\}
} {
  \trho{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\Omega}{\eprog{P}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \thref{thm:progprog} (program progress) on (1), either
(2) $\pstep{\D}{P}{P'}{\D'}$ or $P = \eret{v}$.
\item In the former case, we apply \ts{ER-Prog} to step as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Prog] {
  \pstep{\D}{P}{P'}{\D'}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\eprog{P}}{\D'}{m}{\eprog{P'}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item In the latter case, we can apply \ts{ER-PDone}, defining $\D'$
and $m'$ as in the premise:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-PDone] {
  \D' = \D[\C_0 \mapsto x_0.\eret{v}]
  \\
  m' = m[p_0 \mapsto \{\mf{spec}: \pair{\C_0}{()}, \mf{st}: \cdot,
  \mf{tree}: \cdot, \mf{rerender}: \mf{ff}\}]
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\eprog{\eret{v}}}{\D'}{m'}{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p_0}{\einit{v}}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, in either case we can step, and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Init}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Init] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\tval{\cdot}{\Pi}{v}{\tview}}
} {
  \trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\einit{v}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \thref{thm:canon} (canonical forms) on (1),
we have three subcases: either (a) $v=\vtxt{v'}$, 
(b) $v=\vtag{v_1}{v_2}$, or (c) $v=\pair{\C}{v'}$.
\item In subcase (a), \ts{ER-IText} gives
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\einit{\vtxt{v'}}}{\D}{m}{\elit{v'}}$.
\item In subcase (b), we apply \ts{ER-ITag}
(with some fresh $\mf{id}$) to get that
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\einit{\vtag{v_1}{v_2}}}
{\D}{m}{\enode{id}{v_1}{\einits{v_2}}}$.
\item In subcase (c), we want to apply \ts{ER-ISpec},
which says \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-ISpec] {
  p\ \mf{fresh}
  \and 
  \D[\C]=x.\hat{e}
  \\
  m'=m[p \mapsto \{
    \mf{spec}=\pair{\C}{v'}, 
    \mf{st}: \cdot, 
    \mf{tree}: \cdot, 
    \mf{rerender}: \mf{ff}
  \}]
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\einit{\pair{\C}{v'}}}
  {\D}{m'}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}[v'/x]}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item We choose some fresh $p$ and choose $m'$ as in the premise;
it suffices to show that $\D[\C]=x.\hat{e}$.
Note that, by definition of the original typing judgment, 
we must have $\Omega = \dom(\Delta)$,
and by premise (i), we get $\Omega = \dom(\D)$.
Therefore, it suffices to show (2) that $\C \in \Delta$.
\item To show (2), we perform further inversion on (1):
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Spec] {
  \inferrule* {(3)} {\tval{\cdot}{\Pi}{v}{\Delta[\C]}}
} {
  \tval{\cdot}{\Pi}{\pair{\C}{v}}{\mf{View}}\ (1)
}   
\end{mathpar}
\item For (3) to hold, we must have $\C \in \Delta$, i.e. (2) holds.
\item We have thus shown that $\trip{\D}{m}{\einit{v}}$
steps in any case, and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Inits}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Inits] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\tval{\cdot}{\Pi}{v}{[\tview]}}
} {
  \trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\einits{v}}{[\ttree]}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \thref{thm:canon} (canonical forms) on (1), we have that
either (a) $v=[]$ or (b) $v = v' :: vs$.
\item In subcase (a), we apply \ts{ER-INil} to get
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\einits{[]}}{\D}{m}{[]}$.
\item In subcase (b), we apply \ts{ER-ICons} to get that
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\einits{v :: vs}}{\D}{m}{\einit{v} :: \einits{vs}}$.
\item Thus, in any case we take a step and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-EvalI}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\tbod{\cdot}{\Pi}{\hat{e}}{\Pi[p]}{\Sigma}}
  \and
  \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]]) = \Sigma \cup \Pi[p].\mf{labs}
} {
  \trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We apply \thref{thm:progbodi} (body progress at $\phi=\mf{Init}$)
using (1) and premise (ii) to get that either 
(a) $\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}$
or (b) $\hat{e}=\eret{v}$.
\item In subcase (a), we apply \ts{ER-Eval} as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Eval] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(a)}] { } {
    \bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}
  }
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}}}
  {\D}{m'}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}'}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item In subcase (b), we apply rule \ts{ER-New} to get
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\eret{v}}}
{\D}{m}{\enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{\einit{v}}}$.
\item In either case, we take a step and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-EvalS}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalS] {
  \inferrule*{(1)} {\tbod{\cdot}{\Pi}{\hat{e}}{\Pi[p]}{\Sigma}}
  \
  \inferrule*{(2)} {\Sigma \subseteq \Pi[p].\mf{labs}}
  \
  \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
} {
  \trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We apply \thref{thm:progbods} to (1), (2),
and premise (ii), getting that either 
$\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}$
or $\hat{e}=\eret{v}$.
\item We now continue analogously to the previous case:
we step via either \ts{ER-Eval} or \ts{ER-New} respectively.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-New}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-New] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\rho}{\ttree}}
  \and
  \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
} {
  \trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\enew{\phi}{p}{\rho}}{\rtp(\phi)}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We first apply the inductive hypothesis to (1),
getting that either
(a) $\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}$
or (b) $\rho = u$.
\item In subcase (a), we apply \ts{ER-Ctx} 
with $\E=\enew{\phi}{p}{[\cdot]}$ to step as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Ctx] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(a)}] { } {\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\enew{\phi}{p}{\rho}}{\D'}{m'}{\enew{\phi}{p}{\rho'}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item In subcase (b), we apply \ts{ER-NewI} or \ts{ER-NewS}
when $\phi=\mf{Init}$ or $\phi=\mf{Succ}$ respectively:
\begin{align*}
\cstep{\D}{m}{\enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{u}&}{\D}{m \mid m[p].\mf{tree} = u}{p}
&&\ts{ER-NewI}\\
\cstep{\D}{m}{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p}{u}&}{\D}{m \mid m[p].\mf{tree} = u}{()}
&&\ts{ER-NewS}
\end{align*}
\item Thus, in any case we take a step and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Sr}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Sr] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\rho}{\ttree}}
} {
  \trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\esearch{id}{\rho}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1), either 
(a) $\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}$
or (b) $\rho=u$.
\item In subcase (a), we step via \ts{ER-Ctx} at $\E=\esearch{id}{[\cdot]}$
as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Ctx] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(a)}] { } {\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\esearch{id}{\rho}}{\D'}{m'}{\esearch{id}{\rho'}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item In subcase (b), we apply \thref{thm:canonrt} (canonical forms)
to get three more subcases: either (b-i) $u=\elit{v}$,
(b-ii) $u=\enode{id'}{v}{u}$, or (b-iii) $u=p$.
\item In subcase (b-i), we step via \ts{ER-SLit}:
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\esearch{id}{\elit{v}}}{\D}{m}{()}$.
\item In subcase (b-ii), we step via \ts{ER-SNodeT}
or \ts{ER-SNodeF} when $\mf{id} = \mf{id}'$ or not respectively:
\end{itemize}
\begin{align*}
\cstep{\D}{m}{\esearch{id}{\enode{id}{v}{\rho}}&}
{\D}{m}{\escans{v} \semi \esearches{id}{\rho}}
&&\ts{ER-SNodeT}\\
\cstep{\D}{m}{\esearch{id}{\enode{id'}{v}{\rho}}&}
{\D}{m}{\esearches{id}{\rho}}\ (\mf{id} \neq \mf{id}')
&&\ts{ER-SNodeF}
\end{align*}
\begin{itemize}
\item In subcase (b-iii), we gain additional information from (1):
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Path] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt}
  \and
  \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
} {
  \trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{p}{\ttree}\ (1)
}
\end{mathpar}
\item By (2) and premise (ii), we get 
that $\trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{m[p].\mf{tree}}{\ttree}$,
which implies that $m[p].\mf{tree}$ is well-defined.
We can therefore apply \ts{ER-SPath} to step:
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\esearch{id}{p}}{\D}{m}{\esearch{id}{m[p].\mf{tree}}}$.
\item Thus, in any subcase, we take a step and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Srs}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Srs] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\rho}{[\ttree]}}
} {
  \trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\esearches{id}{\rho}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1), either 
(a) $\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}$
or (b) $\rho=u$.
\item In subcase (a), we step via \ts{ER-Ctx} at $\E=\esearches{id}{[\cdot]}$
as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Ctx] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(a)}] { } {\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\esearches{id}{\rho}}{\D'}{m'}{\esearches{id}{\rho'}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item In subcase (b), \thref{thm:canonrt} (canonical forms) gives us
two further subcases: either (b-i) $u=[]$ or (b-ii) $u=u' :: us$.
\item In subcase (b-i), \ts{ER-SNil} gives that
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\esearches{id}{[]}}{\D}{m}{()}$.
\item In subcase (b-ii), \ts{ER-SCons} gives us that
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\esearches{id}{\rho :: \rho s}}
{\D}{m}{\esearch{id}{\rho} \semi \esearches{id}{\rho s}}$.
\item Thus, in any subcase, we take a step and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Sc}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Sc] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\tval{\cdot}{\Pi}{v}{\tattr}}
} {
  \trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{\escan{v}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \thref{thm:canon} (canonical forms) on (1),
either (a) $v=\vattr{v_1}{v_2}$ or (b) $v=\vclick{v'}$.
\item In subcase (a), \ts{ER-ScAttr} gives
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\escan{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}}}{\D}{m}{()}$.
\item In subcase (b), \ts{ER-ScClick} gives us that
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\escan{\vclick{v}}}{\D}{m}{\ehdl{v\ ()}}$.
\item Thus, we step in any case and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Scs}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Scs] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{[\tattr]}}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\escans{v}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \thref{thm:canon} (canonical forms) on (1), we have that
either (a) $v=[]$ or (b) $v = v' :: vs$.
\item In subcase (a), we apply \ts{ER-ScNil} to get
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\escans{[]}}{\D}{m}{()}$.
\item In subcase (b), we apply \ts{ER-ScCons} to get that
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\escans{v :: vs}}{\D}{m}{\escan{v} \semi \escans{vs}}$.
\item Thus, in any case we take a step and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Hdl}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Hdl] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\texp{\Gamma}{\Pi}{e}{\tone}{E}}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\ehdl{e}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \thref{thm:progexp} (expression progress) on (1)
and premise (ii), either (a) $e=\eret{v}$ or (b) $\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m'}$.
\item In subcase (a), \ts{ER-HDone} gives
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\ehdl{\eret{()}}}{\D}{m}{()}$.
\item In subcase (b), we step via \ts{ER-Hdl} as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Hdl] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(b)}] { } {\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m'}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\ehdl{e}}{\D}{m'}{\ehdl{e'}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, in either subcase, we take a step and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Chk}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Chk] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho}{\ttree}}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\echk{\rho}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1), either 
(a) $\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}$
or (b) $\rho=u$.
\item In subcase (a), we step via \ts{ER-Ctx} at $\E=\echk{[\cdot]}$
as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Ctx] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(a)}] { } {\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\echk{\rho}}{\D'}{m'}{\echk{\rho'}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item In subcase (b), we apply \thref{thm:canonrt} (canonical forms)
to get three more subcases: either (b-i) $u=\elit{v}$,
(b-ii) $u=\enode{id}{v}{u}$, or (b-iii) $u=p$.
\item In subcase (b-i), we step via \ts{ER-ChkLit}:
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\echk{\elit{v}}}{\D}{m}{()}$.
\item In subcase (b-ii), \ts{ER-ChkNode} gives that
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\echk{\enode{id}{v}{\rho}}}{\D}{m}{\echks{\rho}}$.
\item In subcase (b-iii), we continue inversion from (1):
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Path] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt}
  \and
  \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{p}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item By (2) and premise (ii), we have 
that $\trhos{\cdot}{\Pi}{m[p].\mf{tree}}{\ttree}$,
i.e. $m[p].\mf{tree}$ is well-defined.
\item We subcase once more on whether 
$m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff$ or $m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt$.
In the latter case, we can now step via \ts{ER-ChkPF}:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkPF] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\echk{p}}{\D}{m}{\echk{m[p].\mf{tree}}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item If instead $m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt$, we want to 
apply \ts{ER-ChkPT} as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkPT] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt
  \and
  m[p].\mf{spec} = \pair{\C}{v}
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {\D[\C] = x.\hat{e}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\echk{p}}
  {\D}{m \mid m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff}{\eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, it suffices to show (3), that $\D[\C]$ is well-defined.
Note that, by definition of the original typing judgment, 
we must have $\Omega = \dom(\Delta)$,
and by premise (i), we get $\Omega = \dom(\D)$.
Therefore, it suffices to show (4) that $\C \in \Delta$.
\item By premise (ii), we get that $\tval{\cdot}{\Pi}{v}{\Delta[\C]}$,
implying $\C \in \Delta$, concluding the subcase.
\item We can thus take a step in any subcase, and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Chks}: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Chks] {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho}{[\ttree]}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\echks{\rho}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1), either 
(a) $\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}$
or (b) $\rho=u$.
\item In subcase (a), we step via \ts{ER-Ctx} at $\E=\echks{[\cdot]}$
as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Ctx] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(a)}] { } {\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\echks{\rho}}{\D'}{m'}{\echks{\rho'}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item In subcase (b), \thref{thm:canonrt} (canonical forms) gives us
two further subcases: either (b-i) $u=[]$ or (b-ii) $u=u' :: us$.
\item In subcase (b-i), \ts{ER-ChkNil} gives that
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\echks{[]}}{\D}{m}{()}$.
\item In subcase (b-ii), \ts{ER-ChkCons} gives us that
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\echks{\rho :: \rho s}}
{\D}{m}{\echk{\rho} \semi \echks{\rho s}}$.
\item Thus, in any subcase, we take a step and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\end{proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[Preservation of configurations.]
\label{thm:prezcfg}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\tcfg{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\Omega}{\D}{m}{\rho}{\rtp}$ and
\item $\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}$,
\end{enumerate}
then, for some $\Pi' \rpodl \Pi$ and $\Omega' \rpodl \Omega$,
we have $\tcfg{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\Omega'}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}{\rtp}$.

\begin{Proof}
Premise \emph{(i)} gives us the following three sub-premises:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(i-\alph*)]
\item $\tdef{\Gamma}{\Omega}{\D}$,
\item $\tmem{\Gamma}{\Pi}{m}$, and
\item $\trho{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\Omega}{\rho}{\rtp}$.
\end{enumerate}

Likewise, we want to show the following three sub-conclusions:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\tdef{\Gamma}{\Omega'}{\D'}$,
\item $\tmem{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{m'}$, and
\item $\trho{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\Omega'}{\rho'}{\rtp}$.
\end{enumerate}

Unless otherwise specified, we choose $\Omega'=\Omega$
and $\Pi'=\Pi$, such that conclusions (i)
and (ii) are immediate from premises (i-a) and (i-b)
when $\D'=\D$ and $m'=m$, respectively.

As with progress, we work with sub-judgment
$\trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho}{\rtp}$ unless $\Omega$ is relevant.

We proceed by induction over the step judgment in premise \emph{(ii)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-Ctx}: We have \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Ctx] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\cstep{\D}{m}{\rho}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\E[\rho]}{\D'}{m'}{\E'[\rho]}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Premise \emph{(i)} says that 
$\tcfg{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\Omega}{\D}{m}{\E[\rho]}{\rtp}$.
\item \thref{thm:holecfg} (hole typing) on \emph{(i)} gives us
(2) that $\tcfg{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\Omega}{\D}{m}{\rho}{\rtp'}$.
\item We apply the inductive hypothesis to (1) and (2) to get
some $\Pi' \rpodl \Pi$ and $\Omega' \rpodl \Omega$ such that
(3) $\tcfg{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\Omega'}{\D'}{m'}{\rho'}{\rtp'}$.
\item Finally, we apply \thref{thm:replcfg} (replacement) to
premise \emph{(i)}, substituting (3) in for (2),
to get that $\tcfg{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\Omega'}{\D'}{m'}{\E[\rho']}{\rtp}$
and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-Seq}:
We have $\cstep{\D}{m}{() \semi \rho}{\D}{m}{\rho}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Seq] {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{()}{\tone}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho}{\rtp}}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{() \semi \rho}{\rtp}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Conclusion (iii) is directly given by (1) and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-Prog}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Prog] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\pstep{\D}{P}{P'}{\D'}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\eprog{P}}{\D'}{m}{\eprog{P'}}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Prog] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\tprog{\Gamma}{P}{\Omega}}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(3)} {\Delta[\C_0] = \pair{\tone}{\emptyset}}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(4)} {\dom(\Delta) = \Omega \cup \Omega_D \cup \{\C_0\}}
} {
  \trho{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\Omega_D}{\eprog{P}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Premise (i), in this case,
says (5) that $\tdef{\Gamma}{\Omega_D}{\D}$.
We apply \thref{thm:prezprog} to (1), (2), and (5) 
to get some $\Omega' \podl \Omega$
and $\Omega_D' \rpodl \Omega_D$ such that:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\alph*)]
\item $\tprog{\Gamma}{P'}{\Omega'}$,
\item $\tdef{\Gamma}{\Omega_D'}{\D'}$, and
\item $\Omega' \cup \Omega_D' = \Omega \cup \Omega_D$.
\end{enumerate}
\item Choose our new $\Omega$ to be $\Omega_D'$, such that
conclusion (i) is given by (b).
\item By (4) and (c), we get (6) that 
$\dom(\Delta) = \Omega' \cup \Omega_D' \cup \{\C_0\}$.
We derive conclusion (iii) as follows, and we are done:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Prog] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(a)}] { } {\tprog{\Gamma}{P'}{\Omega'}}
  \
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(3)}] { } {\Delta[\C_0] = \pair{\tone}{\emptyset}}
  \:
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(6)}] { } {
    \dom(\Delta) = \Omega' \cup \Omega_D' \cup \{\C_0\}
  }
} {
  \trho{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\Omega_D'}{\eprog{P'}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-PDone}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-PDone] {
  \D' = \D[\C_0 \mapsto x.\eret{v}]
  \\
  m' = m[p_0 \mapsto \{\mf{spec}: \pair{\C_0}{()}, \mf{st}: \cdot,
  \mf{tree}: \cdot, \mf{rerender}: \mf{ff}\}]
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\eprog{\eret{v}}}{\D'}{m'}{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p_0}{\einit{v}}}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Prog] {
  \inferrule*{
    (1)
  } {
    \tprog{\Gamma}{\eret{v}}{\emptyset}
  }
  \
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Delta[\C_0] = \pair{\tone}{\emptyset}}
  \
  \inferrule*{(3)} {
    \dom(\Delta) = \emptyset \cup \Omega \cup \{\C_0\}
  }
} {
  \trho{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\Omega}{\eprog{\eret{v}}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item From (1) we further derive:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TP-Lift] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
    \inferrule* {(4)} {\tval{\Gamma}{\cdot}{v}{\tview}}
  } {
    \texp{\Gamma}{\cdot}{\eret{v}}{\tview}{\epsilon}
  }
} {
  \tprog{\Gamma}{\eret{v}}{\emptyset}\ (1)
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Choose $\Omega' = \Omega \cup \C_0$.
To show conclusion (i) given premise (i-a), 
it thus suffices to show (5)
that $\tbod{\Gamma[x_0 \mapsto \Delta[\C_0]]}{\cdot}
{\eret{v}}{\C_0}{\dom(\Delta[\C_0])}$.
\item By freshness of $x_0$, $\Gamma \po \Gamma[x_0 \mapsto \tone]$.
We can thus apply \thref{thm:wkgav} (weakening) to (4)
to get (6) that $\tval{\Gamma[x_0 \mapsto \tone]}{\cdot}{v}{\tview}$.
(5) is then given by (2) and the following derivation:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
    \inferrule*[right=\tn{(6)}] { } {
      \tval{\Gamma[x_0 \mapsto \tone]}{\cdot}{v}{\tview}
    }
  } {
    \texp{\Gamma[x_0 \mapsto \tone]}{\cdot}{\eret{v}}{\tview}{\epsilon}
  }
} {
  \tbod{\Gamma[x_0 \mapsto \tone]}{\cdot}{\eret{v}}{\C_0}{\emptyset}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Choose $\Pi'=\Pi[p_0 \mapsto \{
  \mf{spec}: \C_0,
  \mf{labs}: \emptyset,
  \mf{tree}: \ff
\}]$. Since $p_0$ is fresh, we have $\Pi \podl \Pi'$.
Then by definition of $m'$, to show conclusion (ii),
it suffices to show that $\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{()}{\Delta[\C_0]}$.
This is immediate from (2) and \ts{TV-Unit}.
\item Finally, we want to show conclusion (iii).
By (3) and definition of $\Omega'$, we have $\dom(\Delta)=\Omega'$,
so it suffices to show (7) 
$\trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p_0}{\einit{v}}}{\tone}$.
\item We weaken (4) by \thref{thm:wkpiv} to get (8) that
$\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{v}{\tview}$.
Also, by (4) and definition of $\Pi'$,
we have (9) that $\Pi'[p_0].\mf{labs} = \emptyset = \dom(\Delta[\Pi'[p_0]])$.
We can now derive (7) as follows, and we are done:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-New] {
  \inferrule*[right=TR-Init] {
    \inferrule*[right=\tn{(8)}] { } {\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{v}{\tview}}
  } {
    \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\einit{v}}{\ttree}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(9)}] { } {
    \Pi'[p_0].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi'[p_0]])
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p_0}{\einit{v}}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-ISpec}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-ISpec] {
  p\ \mf{fresh}
  \and 
  \D[\C]=x.\hat{e}
  \\
  m'=m[p \mapsto \{
    \mf{spec}=\pair{\C}{v}, 
    \mf{st}: \cdot, 
    \mf{tree}: \cdot, 
    \mf{rerender}: \mf{ff}
  \}]
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\einit{\pair{\C}{v}}}{\D}{m'}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]}}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Init] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Spec] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\Delta[\C]}}
  } {
    \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\pair{\C}{v}}{\tview}
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\einit{\pair{\C}{v}}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Choose $\Pi'=\Pi[p \mapsto \{
  \mf{spec}: \C,
  \mf{labs}: \emptyset,
  \mf{tree}: \ff
\}]$. $p$ is fresh, so $\Pi \podl \Pi'$.
\item To show conclusion (ii) given premise (i-b),
it suffices to show $\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{v}{\Delta[\C]}$.
This is immediate from (1) and \thref{thm:wkpiv} (weakening).
\item By premise (i-a), we get
$\tbod{\Gamma[x \mapsto \Delta[\C]]}{\cdot}{\hat{e}}{\C}{\dom(\Delta[\C])}$.
Say $\Sigma = \dom(\Delta[\C])$.
We apply lemmas as follows to get (2):
\begin{align*}
&\tbod{\Gamma[x \mapsto \Delta[\C]]}{\cdot}{\hat{e}}{\C}{\Sigma}
&&(\tn{Premise (i-a)})\\
\Rightarrow\quad
&\tbod{\Gamma[x \mapsto \Delta[\C]]}{\Pi}{\hat{e}}{\C}{\Sigma}
&&(\tn{\thref{thm:wkpib}, weakening})\\
\Rightarrow\quad
&\tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\hat{e}[v/x]}{\C}{\Sigma}
&&(\tn{\thref{thm:subbod}, substitution with (1)})\\
\Rightarrow\quad&(2)\quad
\tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\hat{e}[v/x]}{\C}{\Sigma}
&&(\tn{\thref{thm:wkpib}, weakening})
\end{align*}
\item Since $\Pi'[p].\mf{labs} = \emptyset$,
we also have (3) that 
$\dom(\Delta[\Pi'[p]]) = \Sigma \cup \Pi'[p].\mf{labs}$.
We can now derive conclusion (iii) as follows, 
noting that $\Pi'[p]=\C$, and we are done:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\hat{e}[v/x]}{\C}{\Sigma}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \dom(\Delta[\C]) = \Sigma \cup \Pi'[p].\mf{labs}
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-IText}:
We have $\cstep{\D}{m}{\einit{\vtxt{v}}}{\D}{m}{\elit{v}}$
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Init] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Text] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tstr}}
  } {
    \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\vtxt{v}}{\mf{View}}
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\einit{\vtxt{v}}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We derive conclusion (iii) as follows, and we are done:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Lit] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(1)}] { } {\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tstr}}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\elit{v}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}

} {

}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}

\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Node] {

  \and
  \inferrule*[right=TR-Inits] {
    \inferrule*[right=\tn{(2)}] { } {
      \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v_2}{[\mf{View}]}
    }
  } {
    \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\einits{v_2}}{[\ttree]}
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\enode{id}{v_1}{\einits{v_2}}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}

} {

}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-Eval}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Eval] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}}
} {
  \cstep{\D}{m}{\eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}}}{\D}{m'}{\eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}'}}
}
\end{mathpar}
We subcase on whether $\phi=\mf{Init}$ or $\phi=\mf{Succ}$.

If $\phi=\mf{Init}$, we also have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\hat{e}}{\Pi[p]}{\Sigma}}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(3)} {\dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]]) = \Sigma \cup \Pi[p].\mf{labs}}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We apply \thref{thm:prezbodi} (body preservation at $\phi=\mf{Init}$)
using (1), (2), and premise (i-b) 
to get some $\Pi' \rpodl \Pi$ and $\Sigma' \subseteq \Sigma$ such that:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\alph*)]
\item $\tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\hat{e}'}{\Pi'[p]}{\Sigma'}$,
\item $\tmem{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{m'}$, and
\item $\Pi'[p].\mf{labs} \cup \Sigma' = \Pi[p].\mf{labs} \cup \Sigma$.
\end{enumerate}
\item Choose this $\Pi'$ as our new $\Pi$. 
Conclusion (ii) is immediate from (b).
\item By (c) and (3), we get (4) that
$\dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]]) = \Sigma' \cup \Pi'[p].\mf{labs}$.
Since $\Pi \podl \Pi'$, it must be that 
$\Pi[p].\mf{spec}=\Pi'[p].\mf{spec}$,
so we conclude this subcase by deriving conclusion (iii) as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(1)}] { } {
    \tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\hat{e}'}{\Pi'[p]}{\Sigma'}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \dom(\Delta[\Pi'[p]]) = \Sigma' \cup \Pi'[p].\mf{labs}
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}'}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
If instead $\phi=\mf{Succ}$, we have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalS] {
  \inferrule* {(5)} {\tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\hat{e}}{\Pi[p]}{\Sigma}}
  \
  \inferrule* {(6)} {\Sigma \subseteq \Pi[p].\mf{labs}}
  \
  \inferrule* {(7)} {\Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item It follows from \thref{thm:progbods}
(body progress at $\phi=\mf{Succ}$),
applied to (5), (6), and premise (i-b), that $m' = m$. 
Choose $\Pi'=\Pi$; conclusion (ii) is trivial.
\item We apply \thref{thm:prezbods} 
(body preservation at $\phi=\mf{Succ}$)
using (1), (5), and premise (i-b) 
to get some $\Sigma' \subseteq \Sigma$ with
(8) $\tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\hat{e}'}{\C}{\Sigma'}$.
\item Since $\Sigma' \subseteq \Sigma$,
it follows from (6) and (7) that (9)
$\Sigma' \subseteq \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])$.
We can now derive conclusion (iii) as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalS] {
  \inferrule*[right=(6)] { } {
    \tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\hat{e}'}{\Pi[p]}{\Sigma}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=(9)] { } {
    \Sigma \subseteq \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}'}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-New}: We have
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\eeval{\phi}{p}{\eret{v}}}
{\D}{m}{\enew{\phi}{p}{\einit{v}}}$.

We again subcase on whether $\phi=\mf{Init}$ or $\phi=\mf{Succ}$.

If $\phi=\mf{Init}$, we have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\eret{v}}{\Pi[p]}{\emptyset}
  }
  \
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]]) = \emptyset \cup \Pi[p].\mf{labs}}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\eret{v}}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We continue inversion on (1):
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
    \inferrule* {(3)} {
      \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tview}
    }
  } {
    \texp{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\eret{v}}{\tview}{\epsilon}
  }
} {
  \tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\eret{v}}{\Pi[p]}{\emptyset}\ (1)
}
\end{mathpar}
\item We now derive conclusion (iii) as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-New] {
  \inferrule*[right=TR-Init] {
    \inferrule*[right=\tn{(3)}] { } {
      \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tview}
    }
  } {
    \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\einit{v}}{\ttree}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(2)}] { } {
    \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{\einit{v}}}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
\end{itemize}
If instead $\phi=\mf{Succ}$, we have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalS] {
  \inferrule* {(4)} {
    \tbod{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\eret{v}}{\Pi[p]}{\emptyset}
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(5)} {
    \emptyset \subseteq \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item (4) is identical to (1), so we perform the same inversion
to obtain (3) in this subcase.
\item We derive conclusion (iii) as follows, 
thus satisfying all subcases:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-New] {
  \inferrule*[right=TR-Init] {
    \inferrule*[right=\tn{(3)}] { } {
      \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tview}
    }
  } {
    \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\einit{v}}{\ttree}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(5)}] { } {
    \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\einit{v}}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-NewI}: We have
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{u}}
{\D}{m'}{p}$
with $m'$ defined to be $m \mid m[p].\mf{tree} = u$,
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-New] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{u}{\ttree}
  }
  \

} {

}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Choose $\Pi' = \Pi \mid \Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt$.
By (2), we already have $p \in \dom(\Pi)$,
so to show conclusion (ii), it suffices to show
that $\trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{u}{\ttree}$, which is given by (1).
\item Conclusion (iii) follows from the derivation:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Path] {
  \inferrule* { } {\Pi'[p].\mf{tree} = \tt}
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \Pi'[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi'[p]])
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi'}{p}{\ttree}
}
\end{mathpar}
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-NewS}: We have

where $m'$ is defined as $m \mid m[p].\mf{tree} = u$,
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-New] {

  \and

} {

\begin{itemize}
\item Choose $\Pi' = \Pi \mid \Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt$.

that $\trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{u}{\ttree}$, which is given by (1).
\item Conclusion (iii) is immediate by \ts{TR-Unit}
and we are done.

\inferrule*[right=TR-Seq] {

    }
  } {

  \

    }

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-SNil}: We have
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\esearches{id}{[]}}{\D}{m}{()}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Srs] {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{[]}{[\ttree]}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\esearches{id}{[]}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Conclusion (iii) is immediate from \ts{TR-Unit},
and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-SCons}: We have\\
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\esearches{id}{\rho :: \rho s}}
{\D}{m}{\esearch{id}{\rho} \semi \esearches{id}{\rho s}}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Srs] {
  \inferrule*[right=TR-Cons] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {\trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho}{\ttree}}
    \and
    \inferrule* {(2)} {\trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho s}{[\ttree]}}
  } {
    \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho :: \rho s}{[\ttree]}
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\esearches{id}{\rho :: \rho s}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We derive conclusion (iii) as follows and we are done:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Seq] {
  \inferrule*[right=TR-Sr] {
    \inferrule*[right=\tn{(1)}] { } {
      \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho}{\ttree}
    }
  } {
    \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\esearch{id}{\rho}}{\tone}
  }
  \inferrule*[right=TR-Srs] {
    \inferrule*[right=\tn{(2)}] { } {
      \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho s}{[\ttree]}
    }
  } {
    \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\esearches{id}{\rho s}}{\tone}
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}
  {\esearch{id}{\rho} \semi \esearches{id}{\rho s}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-ScAttr}: We have
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\escan{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}}}{\D}{m}{()}$
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Sc] {
  \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}}{\tattr}
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\escan{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}

and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-ScClick}: We have
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\escan{\vclick{v}}}{\D}{m}{\ehdl{v\ ()}}$
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Sc] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Click] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {
      \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tone \arrow{E} \tone}
    }
  } {
    \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\vclick{v}}{\tattr}
  }
} {
  \trhos{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\escan{\vclick{v}}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We derive conclusion (iii) as follows and we are done:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Hdl] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-AppL] {
    \inferrule*[right=(1)] { } {
      \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tone \arrow{E} \tone}
    }
    \
    \inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
      \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{()}{\tone}
    }
  } {

  }
} {

}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-ScNil}: We have
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\escans{[]}}{\D}{m}{()}$ and:

} {

and we are done.

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-ScCons}: We have
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\escans{v :: vs}}
{\D}{m}{\escan{v} \semi \escans{vs}}$
and: \begin{mathpar}

} {

\item We derive conclusion (iii) as follows and we are done:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Seq] {

} {
