\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TL-Chks] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(1)}] { } {
    \trhoi{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho}{[\ttree]}
  }
} {
  \trhol{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\cdot}{\echks{\rho}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-ChkNil}: 
We have $\cstep{\D}{m}{\echks{[]}}{\D}{m}{()}$
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TL-Chks] {
  \trhoi{\Gamma}{\Pi}{[]}{[\ttree]}
} {
  \trhol{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\cdot}{\echks{[]}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Conclusion (iii) is immediate from \ts{TL-Unit},
and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{ER-ChkCons}: We have\\
$\cstep{\D}{m}{\echks{\rho :: \rho s}}
{\D}{m}{\echk{\rho} \semi \echks{\rho s}}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TL-Chks] {
  \inferrule*[right=TI-Cons] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {\trhoi{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho}{\ttree}}
    \and
    \inferrule* {(2)} {\trhoi{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho s}{[\ttree]}}
  } {
    \trhoi{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\cdot}{\rho :: \rho s}{[\ttree]}
  }
} {
  \trhol{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\cdot}{\echks{\rho :: \rho s}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We derive conclusion (iii) as follows and we are done:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TL-Seq] {
  \inferrule*[right=TL-Chk] {
    \inferrule*[right=\tn{(1)}] { } {
      \trhoi{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho}{\ttree}
    }
  } {
    \trhol{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\cdot}{\echk{\rho}}{\tone}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=TL-Chks] {
    \inferrule*[right=\tn{(2)}] { } {
      \trhoi{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\rho s}{[\ttree]}
    }
  } {
    \trhol{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\cdot}{\echks{\rho s}}{\tone}
  }
} {
  \trhol{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\cdot}{\echk{\rho} \semi \echks{\rho s}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\hl{TODO}: we need to show expression and body evaluations
don't affect $\Theta$ (i.e. linear memory typing is preserved).
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\subsection{Realization}

We define the $\mf{realize}$ meta-function to ``unfold'' a memory into
a concrete tree structure as follows:
\begin{align*}
\mf{realize}_m(\elit{v}) &= \elit{v}\\
\mf{realize}_m(\enode{id}{v}{u}) &= \enode{id}{v}{\mf{realize}_m(u)}\\
\mf{realize}_m(p) &= \mf{realize}_m(m[p].\mf{tree})\\
\mf{realize}_m([]) &= []\\
\mf{realize}_m(u :: us) &= \mf{realize}_m(u) :: \mf{realize}_m(us)
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{Direct Proof Approach}

\hl{...}

\subsubsection{Language-Based Formulation}

\begin{align*}
\text{Concrete tree } t &::= \elit{v} \mid \enode{id}{v}{t}
\mid \mf{realize}(u) \mid [] \mid t :: t\\
\text{Concrete tree value } t_v &::= \elit{v} \mid \enode{id}{v}{t_v}
\mid [] \mid t_v :: t_v\\
\text{Concrete tree type } \alpha^* &::= \ttree^* \mid [\ttree]^*\\
\text{Evaluation context } \E &::= [\cdot] \mid \enode{id}{v}{\E}
\mid \E :: t \mid t_v :: \E
\end{align*}

We augment our runtime
tree-typing judgments with a measure $n \in \mathbb{N}$ of the size
of the syntax tree:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TN-Lit] {
  \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tstr}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \cdot \Vdash^1_\Delta \elit{v}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TN-Node] {
  \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{[\tattr]}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta \Vdash^n_\Delta v: [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta \Vdash^{n+1}_\Delta \enode{id}{v}{u}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TN-Path] {
  \Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt
  \and
  \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi p \Vdash^1_\Delta p: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TN-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \cdot \Vdash^1_\Delta []: [\ttree]
}\and
\inferrule*[right=TN-Cons] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_1 \Vdash^{n_1}_\Delta u_1 : \ttree
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_2 \Vdash^{n_2}_\Delta u_2 : [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_1,\Theta_2
  \Vdash^{n_1+n_2+1}_\Delta 
  u_1 :: u_2: [\ttree]
}
\end{mathpar}

We define a typing judgment on concrete trees,
which includes a measure of the number of unrealized syntax nodes:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TC-Lit] {
  \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tstr}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \cdot \Vdash^0_\Delta \elit{v}: \ttree^*
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TC-Node] {
  \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{[\tattr]}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta \Vdash^n_\Delta t: [\ttree]^*
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta \Vdash^{n}_\Delta \enode{id}{v}{t}: \ttree^*
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TC-Real] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta \Vdash^n_\Delta u: \alpha
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta
  \Vdash^{n}_\Delta \mf{realize}(u): \alpha^*
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TC-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \cdot \Vdash^0_\Delta []: [\ttree]^*
}\and
\inferrule*[right=TC-Cons] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_1 \Vdash^{n_1}_\Delta t_1 : \ttree^*
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_2 \Vdash^{n_2}_\Delta t_2 : [\ttree]^*
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_1,\Theta_2
  \Vdash^{n_1+n_2}_\Delta 
  t_1 :: t_2: [\ttree]^*
}
\end{mathpar}

We define $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma \semi \Theta \vDash_\Delta \pi: p$ 
to hold if,
for $\pi.\mf{spec}=\pair{\C}{v}$:
\begin{itemize}
\item $\Pi[p].\mf{spec}=\C$,
\item $\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\Delta[\C]}$,
\item $\dom(\pi.\mf{st}) = \Pi[p].\mf{labs}$,
\item for all $\ell \in \dom(\pi.\mf{st})$,
we have $\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{\pi.\mf{st}[\ell]}{\Delta[\C][\ell]}$,
\item if $\Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt$ and $p \notin \Sigma$, then
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta \Vdash^n_\Delta \pi.\mf{tree} : \ttree$
(for some $n$),
\item if instead $\Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \ff$ or $p \in \Sigma$, then $\Theta=\cdot$, and
\item $p \notin \Theta$.
\end{itemize}
($\Sigma$ here is the context of ``seen'' paths in a realization.)

We define $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma \semi \Theta \vDash_\Delta m$ 
to hold if
there is some series of $\Theta_i$ with $\num{\Theta_i}=\Theta$,
and, for all $p_i \in \dom(m)$,
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma \semi \Theta_i \vDash_\Delta m[p_i]: p_i$.
We also require that $p_0 \notin \Theta$ and $\Theta \cap \Sigma = \emptyset$.

Define 
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma \semi \Theta \vdash_\Delta^n \pair{m}{t}: \alpha$
to abbreviate that 
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma \semi \Theta_m \vDash_\Delta m$
and $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_t \Vdash^n_\Delta t: \alpha$,
where $\Theta=\Theta_m,\Theta_t$.

% TODO: domain-equality constraint? (add to linear definition too?)

Finally, we define an operational semantics to realize concrete trees:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EC-Ctx] {
  t \mapsto_m t'
} {
  \E[t] \mapsto_m \E[t']
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
\mf{realize}(\elit{v}) &\mapsto_m \elit{v} &&\textsc{EC-Lit}\\
\mf{realize}(\enode{id}{v}{u}) &\mapsto_m \enode{id}{v}{\mf{realize}(u)}
&&\textsc{EC-Node}\\
\mf{realize}(p) &\mapsto_m \mf{realize}(m[p].\mf{tree})
&&\textsc{EC-Path}\\
\mf{realize}([]) &\mapsto_m []
&&\textsc{EC-Nil}\\
\mf{realize}(u :: us) &\mapsto_m \mf{realize}(u) :: \mf{realize}(us)
&&\textsc{EC-Cons}\\
\end{align*}

\begin{proposition}[Hole typing, realization.]
\label{thm:realhole}
If $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_e,\Theta_h
\Vdash^{n_e + n_h}_\Delta \E[t]: \alpha^*$,
then $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_h
\Vdash^{n_h}_\Delta t: \alpha'^*$
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Replacement, realization.]
\label{thm:realrepl}
If:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_e,\Theta_h
\Vdash^{n_e + n_h}_\Delta \E[t]: \alpha^*$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_h
\Vdash^{n_h}_\Delta t: \alpha'^*$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_h'
\Vdash^{n_h'}_\Delta t: \alpha'^*$, and
\item $\Theta_e \cap \Theta'_h = \emptyset$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_e,\Theta_h'
\Vdash^{n_e + n_h'}_\Delta \E[t']: \alpha^*$.
\end{proposition}

\begin{lemma}[Progress of realization.]
If $\cdot \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma \semi \Theta 
\vdash^n_\Delta \pair{m}{t}: \alpha$,
then either $t=t_v$ or $t \mapsto_m t'$ for some $t'$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the typing derivation.

The only non-trivial case is when $t=\mf{realize}(p)$,
in which case we need to show that $m[p].\mf{tree}$
is well-defined, but this is given by the premise
that $\Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt$ and the well-typing of the memory $m$.
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[Preservation of realization.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma \semi \Theta
\vdash_\Delta^n \pair{m}{t}: \alpha$ and
\item $t \mapsto_m t'$,
\end{enumerate}
then, for some $\Theta'$, $\Sigma'$, and $n'$, we have
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma' \semi \Theta'
\vdash_\Delta^{n'} \pair{m}{t'}: \alpha$, and
\item either: \begin{enumerate}[label=(ii-\alph*)]
  \item $\Theta' \subset \Theta$, or
  \item $\Theta' = \Theta$ and $n' < n$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the step judgment.

\tb{Case} \ts{EC-Ctx}: (\hl{informal})
\begin{itemize}
\item \thref{thm:realhole}: hole is well-typed
\item and the $\Theta_h$ that types it is disjoint from $\Theta_m$,
so $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma \semi \Theta_m,\Theta_h
\vdash_\Delta^{n_h} \pair{m}{t}: \alpha'$
\item IH gives $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Sigma' \semi \Theta''
\vdash_\Delta^{n'_h} \pair{m}{t'}: \alpha'$
\item subcases: either (a) $\Theta'' \subset \Theta_m,\Theta_h$,
or (b) $\Theta''=\Theta_m,\Theta_h$ and $n'_h < n_h$
\item in subcase (a): since $\Theta_m,\Theta_e,\Theta_h$ is well-typed,
we get $\Theta_m',\Theta_e,\Theta_h'$ is too,
i.e. $\Theta_h' \cap \Theta_e=\emptyset$
\item \thref{thm:realrepl} gives us our conclusion
(choosing $\Theta'=\Theta_m',\Theta_h',\Theta_e$, $\Sigma'$,
and $n'=n_e+n_h'$)
\item in subcase (b), clearly $\Theta_h \cap \Theta_e=\emptyset$
\item so \thref{thm:realrepl} gives us our typing conclusion,
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_e,\Theta_h \Vdash^{n_e+n_h'}_\Delta
\E[t']: \alpha$
\item choose $n'=n_e+n_h'$, satisfying conclusion \emph{(ii-b)}
and we are done
\end{itemize}


\tb{Case} \ts{EC-Lit}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TC-Real] {
  \inferrule*[right=TN-Lit] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tstr}}
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \cdot \Vdash^1_\Delta \elit{v}: \ttree
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \cdot
  \Vdash^{1}_\Delta \mf{realize}(\elit{v}): \ttree^*
}
\end{mathpar}
Choosing $\Theta'=\cdot$, $\Sigma'=\Sigma$, and $n'=0$, 
we derive the conclusion as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TC-Lit] {
  \inferrule*[right=(1)] { } {\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\tstr}}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \cdot \Vdash^0_\Delta \elit{v}: \ttree^*
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{EC-Node}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TC-Real] {
  \inferrule*[right=TN-Node] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{[\tattr]}}
    \and
    \inferrule* {(2)} {
      \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta
      \Vdash^n_\Delta u: [\ttree]
    }
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta 
    \Vdash^{n+1}_\Delta \enode{id}{v}{u}: \ttree
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta
  \Vdash^{n+1}_\Delta \mf{realize}(\enode{id}{v}{u}): \ttree^*
}
\end{mathpar}
Choose $\Theta'=\Theta$, $\Sigma'=\Sigma$,
and $n'=n$ to satisfy conclusion \emph{(ii-b)}.
We derive the typing conclusion as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TC-Node] {
  \inferrule*[right=(1)] { } {
    \tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{[\tattr]}
  }
  \ 
  \inferrule*[right=TC-Real] {
    \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
      \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta
      \Vdash^n_\Delta u: [\ttree]
    }
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta
    \Vdash^n_\Delta \mf{realize}(u): [\ttree]^*
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta
  \Vdash^{n}_\Delta \enode{id}{v}{\mf{realize}(u)}: \ttree^*
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{EC-Path}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TC-Real] {
  \inferrule*[right=TN-Path] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {\Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt}
    \and
    \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \dom(\Delta[\Pi[p]])
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi p \Vdash^1_\Delta p: \ttree
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi p
  \Vdash^{1}_\Delta \mf{realize}(p): \ttree^*
}
\end{mathpar}
Choose $\Theta'=\Theta_m$.
By (1) and the memory typing premise (which implies $p \notin \Sigma$),
there is some split $\Theta_m=\Theta_p,\Theta_m'$
such that (2)
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_p \Vdash^n_\Delta m[p].\mf{tree}: \ttree$.
Split $\Theta'$ such that $\Theta_m'$ is assigned to the memory
and $\Theta_p$ to the term.
Furthermore, choose $\Sigma'=\Sigma,p$ and $n'=n$.
Since $\Theta' \subset \Theta$, we satisfy conclusion \emph{(ii-a)}.

We assign $\Theta_p'=\cdot$ and the rest of $\Theta_m'$ as before,
but the well-typedness
of the memory still holds because $p \in \Sigma$.

Finally, the well-typedness of the term is given as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TC-Real] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_p 
    \Vdash^n_\Delta m[p].\mf{tree}: \ttree
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \Theta_p
  \Vdash^{n}_\Delta \mf{realize}(m[p].\mf{tree}): \ttree^*
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{EC-Nil}: Choose $n'=0$; easy.

\tb{Case} \ts{EC-Cons}: Choose $n'=n-1$; easy.

\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{theorem}[Termination of realization.]
If $\tcfgl{\cdot}{\Pi}{\Theta_m}{\Omega}{\D}{m}{\rho}{\rtp}$
and $\Pi[p_0].\mf{tree} = \tt$,
then $\mf{realize}_m(p_0)$ terminates.

\begin{Proof}
By definition of the typing premise,
we have $\tmeml{\cdot}{\Pi}{\Theta_m}{m}$,
where $p_0 \notin \Theta_m$.
It follows by definition that (1)
$\cdot \semi \Pi \semi \cdot \semi \Theta_m \vDash_\Delta m$.
From the latter premise, 
\hl{as long as $\mf{tree}=\tt \Rightarrow \mf{labs}$ full},
we can derive
(2) that $\cdot \semi \Pi \semi p_0 \Vdash^1_\Delta p_0: \ttree$.
Now, progress and preservation applied repeatedly to (1) and (2)
give us that $\mf{realize}$ never ``gets stuck''.

Choose (lexicographic) measure $\pair{|\Theta|}{n} \in \mathbb{N}^2$.
Preservation tells us that this measure is strictly decreasing
across steps, and since $\mathbb{N}^2$ is lexicographically well-ordered,
$\mf{realize}$ must terminate and we are done.
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\section{Translation Workshopping}

Try: all FGCBV, let-rec-and for components
\begin{align*}
\text{Value } v &::= x \mid () \mid s \mid \lambda x.e \mid [] \mid v :: v\\
&\quad\mid \C \mid \vtxt{v} \mid \vtag{v}{v}
\mid \vattr{v}{v} \mid \vclick{v}\\
&\quad\mid \mgrey{\pair{\C}{v}} \mid \mgrey{\vset{\ell}{p}}\\
\text{Expression } e &::= \eret{v} \mid \elet{x}{e}{e} \mid v\ v \\
&\quad\mid \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}\\
\text{Body }\hat{e} &::= e \mid \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}} \mid \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}\\
\text{Program } P &::= e \mid \elet{x}{e}{P} \mid \elet{\C(x)}{\hat{e}}{P}\\
\text{Type } \ptp &::= \tone \mid \tstr \mid \ptp \arrow{E} \ptp
\mid [\ptp] \\
&\quad\mid \tview \mid \tattr
\mid \tcomp{\ptp} \mid \tset{\ptp}\\
\text{Effect } E &::= \epsilon \mid \emut
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\text{Runtime values } u
&::= ()_\rho \mid []_\rho \mid u ::_\rho u
\mid \elit{v} \mid \enode{id}{v}{u}
\mid p\\
\text{Runtime expressions } \rho
&::= \eret{u} \mid \elet{x}{\rho}{\rho}\\
&\quad\mid
\einit{v} \mid \einits{v} \mid \escan{v} \mid \escans{v}\\
&\quad\mid \echk{u} \mid \echks{u}
\mid \esearch{id}{u} \mid \esearches{id}{u}\\
&\quad\mid \eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}} \mid \enew{\phi}{p}{u}
\mid \ehdl{e} \mid \eprog{P}\\
\text{Runtime type } \rtp &::= \tone_\rho \mid \mf{Tree}
\mid [\ttree]
\end{align*}

\begin{align*}
\text{Target values } v
&::= x \mid () \mid s \mid \lambda x.e \mid [] \mid v :: v \mid \ell\\
&\quad\mid \vtxt{v} \mid \vtag{v}{v} \mid \vattr{v}{v} \mid \vclick{v}\\
&\quad\mid \elit{v} \mid \enode{v}{v}{v}\\
\text{Target expressions } e
&::= \eret{v} \mid \elet{x}{e}{e}
\mid v\ v \mid \eref{v} \mid \eget{v} \mid \eset{v}{v}\\
&\quad\mid \mf{let\ rec}\ \beta\ \mf{in}\ e\\
&\quad\mid \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x :: x}{e_2}\\
&\quad\mid \ecase{v}{\vattr{x}{x}}{e_1}{\vclick{x}}{e_2}\\
&\quad\mid \ecase{v}{\vtxt{x}}{e_1}{\vtag{x}{x}}{e_2}\\
&\quad\mid \ecase{v}{\elit{x}}{e_1}{\enode{x}{x}{x}}{e_2}\\
\text{Binding list } \beta &::= x = v \mid x = v\ \mf{and}\ \beta\\
\text{Target type } A &::= \tone \mid \tstr \mid A \rightarrow A \mid [A]
\mid \tview \mid \tattr \mid \ttree \mid \tref{A}
\end{align*}

\begin{align*}
\llrr{\tcomp{A}} &= A \rightarrow \tref{\tbool} 
\times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)\\
\llrr{\tset{A}} &= A \rightarrow \tone\\
\llrr{\C} &= x_\C\\
\llrr{\pair{\C}{v}} &= \mf{spec}(\lambda ().\llrr{\C}\ \llrr{v})\\
\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}} &= \lambda x.\eset{\ell}{x} 
\semi \mf{flag}_\ell = \mf{true}\\
\end{align*}

\begin{align*}
\llrr{\hat{e}} &= \llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}}\\ \\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}
&= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}}\\
\llrr{e}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0} &= \pair{\mf{flag}}{\lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^\mf{Succ}}\\
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0} 
&= \elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}}}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\lambda y.(\eset{x_\ell}{y} \semi \eset{\mf{flag}}{\mf{true}})}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}}
  }
}
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\llrr{e}^\mf{Succ} &= \llrr{e}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^\mf{Succ} &=
\elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Succ}}\\
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^\mf{Succ} &= 
\elet{x_\mf{set}}{\lambda y.(\eset{x_\ell}{y} \semi \eset{\mf{flag}}{\mf{true}})}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Succ}}
}
\end{align*}

\begin{align*}
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{P}}_\beta &= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{P}_\beta}\\
\llrr{\elet{\C(x)}{\hat{e}}{P}}_\beta &=
\llrr{P}_{\beta, x_\C=\lambda x.\llrr{\hat{e}}_\emptyset}\\
\llrr{e}_\beta &= \mf{let\ rec}\ \beta\ \mf{in}\ \llrr{e}\\
\end{align*}

\begin{align*}
\llrr{\mf{id}} &= s_\mf{id}\\
\llrr{p} &= \ell_p\\
\llrr{\einit{v}} &= \mhl{func}\\
\llrr{\einits{v}} &= \mf{map}\ \mf{init}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\escan{v}} &= \mhl{func}\\
\llrr{\escans{v}} &= \mf{iter}\ \mf{scan}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\echk{v}} &= \mhl{func}\\
\llrr{\echks{v}} &= \mf{iter}\ \mf{check}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\esearch{id}{v}} &= \mhl{func}\\
\llrr{\esearches{id}{v}} &= \mf{iter}\ (\mf{search}\ \llrr{id})\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\ehdl{e}} &= \llrr{e}\\
\llrr{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}}} &= \elet{(flag, s)}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Init}}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{view}{s\ ()}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{tree}{\einit{view}}{\\
      &\quad\ \elet{cell}{\eref{\trip{s}{tree}{flag}}}{
        \mf{Path}(cell)
      }
    }
  }
}\\
\llrr{\enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{u}} &= \elet{tree}{\einit{u}}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{cell}{\eref{\trip{s}{tree}{flag}}}{
    \mf{Path}(cell)
  }
}\\
\llrr{\eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}}} &=
  \elet{view}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Succ}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{tree}{\einit{view}}{\\
      &\quad\ \elet{cell}{\eref{\trip{s}{tree}{flag}}}{
        \mf{Path}(cell)
      }
    }
}\\
\llrr{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p}{u}} &= \mhl{\cdots}
\end{align*}

\section{$\lambda_\mf{cg}$}

A ``coarse-grained'' target calculus, with primitive inductive data types
and destructors alongside ML-style mutable references.

\subsection{Constructor-Abstracted Syntax}

We parametrize our syntax by a set of constructors $C^n$
with arity $n$, and a set of type constants $A_c$.
(We elide $n$ when irrelevant.)
\begin{align*}
\text{Value } v &::= x \mid () \mid s
\mid \erec{f}{x}{e} \mid \ell \mid \mf{id} \mid C^n(v_1,\cdots,v_n)\\
\text{Expression } e &::= v \mid C^n(e_1,\cdots,e_n) \mid e\ e
\mid \eref{e} \mid \eget{e} \mid \eset{e}{e}
\mid \mf{case}\ e\ \{\num{C\ \num{x} \Rightarrow e}\}\\
\text{Type } \ptp &::= \tone \mid \tstr \mid \ptp \rightarrow \ptp \mid \mf{ID} \mid A_c
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\text{Variable context } \Gamma &::= \cdot \mid \Gamma, x: A\\
\text{Store context } \Sigma &::= \cdot \mid \Sigma, x: A\\
\text{Evaluation context } E &::= [\cdot] \mid C(\num{v}, E, \num{e}) 
\mid E\ e \mid v\ E \mid \eref{E}\\
&\quad\mid \eget{E} \mid \eset{E}{e} \mid \eset{v}{E}
\mid \mf{case}\ E\ \{\num{C\ \num{x} \Rightarrow e}\}\\
\text{Store } \sigma &::= \cdot \mid \sigma, \ell \mapsto v
\end{align*}

\subsection{Typing}

We assume the existence of a fixed constructor signature $\Delta$,
which maps constructors to their types (via $\Delta(C)$)
and the types of their $i$th arguments (via $\Delta(C_i)$).

We denote the set of all constructors associated with a type
via the function $\Delta(A)$.

\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=T-Var] { } {
  \Gamma, x: A \semi \Sigma \vdash x: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Str] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash s: \tstr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-ID] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash \mf{id}: \mf{ID}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Fun] {
  \Gamma, f: A_1 \rightarrow A_2, x: A_1 \semi \Sigma \vdash e: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash \erec{f}{x}{e}: A_1 \rightarrow A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Loc] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma, \ell: A \vdash \ell: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Con] {
  (0 \leq i < n)
  \quad
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash v_i: \Delta(C_i)
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash C^n(v_1,\cdots,v_n): \Delta(C)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-App] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash e_1: A_1 \rightarrow A_2
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash e_2: A_1
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash e_1\ e_2: A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Ref] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash e: A
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash \eref{e}: \tref{A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Get] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash e: \tref{A}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash \eget{e}: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Set] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash e_1: \tref{A}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash e_2: A
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash \eset{e_1}{e_2}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Case] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash e: A_1
  \and
  (\forall C^n \in \Delta(A_1)) \quad
  \Gamma, \num{x_j: \Delta(C_j)}
  \semi \Sigma \vdash e_i: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash \mf{case}\ e\ 
  \{\num{C^n_i(x_1,\cdots,x_n) \Rightarrow e_i}\}: A_2
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsection{Operational Semantics}

TBD

\section{$\lambda_\mf{tgt}$}

A ``fine-grained'' target calculus: System F with sums, products, (equi)recursive types,
existentials, and ML-style mutable references.

\subsection{Syntax}

\begin{align*}
\text{Value } v &::= x \mid () \mid s \mid \lambda x.e \mid \Lambda \alpha . v
\mid \epack{A}{v}{\alpha}{A}\\
&\quad\mid \pair{v}{v} \mid \einl{v} \mid \einr{v} \mid \ell \\
\text{Expression } e &::= v \mid \eref{e} \mid \eget{e} \mid \eset{e}{e}
\mid \pair{e}{e} \mid \efst{e} \mid \esnd{e}
\mid \einl{e} \mid \einr{e}\\
&\quad\mid \ecase{e}{\einl{x}}{e}{\einr{x}}{e} \mid e\ e \mid e\ [A]\\
&\quad\mid \epack{A}{e}{\alpha}{A} \mid \eunpack{\alpha}{x}{e}{e}\\
\text{Type } \ptp &::= \alpha \mid \tone \mid \tstr \mid \ptp \rightarrow \ptp
\mid A \times A \mid A + A\\
&\quad\mid \forall \alpha . \ptp \mid \exists \alpha . \ptp
\mid \mu \alpha . \ptp \mid \tref{A}
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\text{Variable context } \Gamma &::= \cdot \mid \Gamma, x: A\\
\text{Type context } \Delta &::= \cdot \mid \Delta, \alpha\\
\text{Store context } \Sigma &::= \cdot \mid \Sigma, x: A\\
\text{Evaluation context } E &::= [\cdot] \mid \eref{E} \mid \eget{E}
\mid \eset{E}{e} \mid \eset{v}{E} \mid \pair{E}{e} \mid \pair{v}{E}\\
&\quad\mid \efst{E} \mid \esnd{E} \mid \einl{E} \mid \einr{E}\\
&\quad\mid \ecase{E}{\einl{x}}{e}{\einr{x}}{e} \mid E\ e \mid v\ E
\mid E\ [A]\\
&\quad\mid \epack{A}{E}{\alpha}{A} \mid \eunpack{\alpha}{x}{E}{e}\\
\text{Store } \sigma &::= \cdot \mid \sigma, \ell \mapsto v
\end{align*}

\subsection{Typing Rules}

Well-formed type judgment: $\ttp{\Delta}{A}$

\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=WF-Var] { } {
  \ttp{\Delta, \alpha}{\alpha}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=WF-One] { } {
  \ttp{\Delta}{\tone}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=WF-Str] { } {
  \ttp{\Delta}{\tstr}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=WF-Fun] {
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_1}
  \and
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_2}
} {
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_1 \rightarrow A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=WF-Prod] {
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_1}
  \and
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_2}
} {
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_1 \times A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=WF-Sum] {
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_1}
  \and
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_2}
} {
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_1 + A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=WF-Forall] {
  \ttp{\Delta,\alpha}{A}
} {
  \ttp{\Delta}{\forall \alpha.A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=WF-Exists] {
  \ttp{\Delta,\alpha}{A}
} {
  \ttp{\Delta}{\exists \alpha.A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=WF-Mu] {
  \ttp{\Delta,\alpha}{A}
  \and
  A \neq \alpha
} {
  \ttp{\Delta}{\mu \alpha.A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=WF-Ref] {
  \ttp{\Delta}{A}
} {
  \ttp{\Delta}{\tref{A}}
}
\end{mathpar}

Typing judgment: $\ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{A}$

\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=T-Var] { } {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma, x: A}{x}{A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Unit] { } {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{()}{\tone}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Str] { } {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{s}{\tstr}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Lam] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma, x: A_1}{e}{A_2}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\lambda x.e}{A_1 \rightarrow A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-App] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e_1}{A_1 \rightarrow A_2}
  \and
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e_2}{A_1}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e_1\ e_2}{A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Loc] { } {
  \ttgt{\Sigma, \ell: A}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\ell}{A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-TLam] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta,\alpha}{\Gamma}{v}{A}
  \and
  \alpha \notin \Delta
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\Lambda \alpha.v}{\forall \alpha.A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-TApp] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{\forall \alpha.A_2}
  \and
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_1}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e\ [A_1]}{A_2[A_1/\alpha]}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Pack] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{A_2[A_1/\alpha]}
  \and
  \ttp{\Delta}{A_1}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\epack{A_1}{e}{\alpha}{A_2}}{\exists \alpha.A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Unpack] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e_1}{\exists \alpha.A_1}
  \and
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta,\alpha}{\Gamma,x:A_1}{e_2}{A_2}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\eunpack{\alpha}{x}{e_1}{e_2}}{A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Fold] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{A[\mu \alpha.A / \alpha]}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{\mu \alpha.A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Unfold] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{\mu \alpha.A}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{A[\mu \alpha.A / \alpha]}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Pair] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e_1}{A_1}
  \and
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e_2}{A_2}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\pair{e_1}{e_2}}{A_1 \times A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Fst] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{A_1 \times A_2}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\efst{e}}{A_1}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Snd] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{A_1 \times A_2}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\esnd{e}}{A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-InL] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{A_1}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\einl{e}}{A_1 + A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-InR] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{A_2}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\einr{e}}{A_1 + A_2}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Case] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e_1}{A_1 + A_2}
  \\
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma,x:A_1}{e_2}{A}
  \and
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma,x:A_2}{e_3}{A}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\ecase{e_1}{\einl{x}}{e_2}{\einr{x}}{e_3}}{A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Ref] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{A}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\eref{e}}{\tref{A}}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Get] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e}{\tref{A}}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\eget{e}}{A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=T-Set] {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e_1}{\tref{A}}
  \and
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{e_2}{A}
} {
  \ttgt{\Sigma}{\Delta}{\Gamma}{\eset{e_1}{e_2}}{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsection{Operational Semantics}

Judgment: $\estep{\sigma}{e}{e'}{\sigma'}$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=E-Ctx] {
  \estep{\sigma}{e}{e'}{\sigma'}
} {
  \estep{\sigma}{E[e]}{E[e']}{\sigma'}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
\estep{\sigma}{(\lambda x.e)\ v&}{e[v/x]}{\sigma}
&&\ts{E-App}\\
\estep{\sigma}{(\Lambda \alpha.v)\ [A]&}{v[A/\alpha]}{\sigma}
&&\ts{E-TApp}\\
\estep{\sigma}{\eunpack{\alpha}{x}{(\epack{A}{v}{\_}{\_})}{e}&}{e[A/\alpha][v/x]}{\sigma}
&&\ts{E-Upp}\\
\estep{\sigma}{\efst{\pair{v_1}{v_2}}&}{v_1}{\sigma}
&&\ts{E-Fst}\\
\estep{\sigma}{\efst{\pair{v_1}{v_2}}&}{v_2}{\sigma}
&&\ts{E-Snd}\\
\estep{\sigma}{\ecase{\einl{v}}{\einl{x}}{e_1}{\einr{x}}{e_2}&}{e_1[v/x]}{\sigma}
&&\ts{E-CaseL}\\
\estep{\sigma}{\ecase{\einr{v}}{\einl{x}}{e_1}{\einr{x}}{e_2}&}{e_2[v/x]}{\sigma}
&&\ts{E-CaseR}\\
(\ell \notin \dom(\sigma))\qquad
\estep{\sigma}{\eref{v}&}{\ell}{\sigma,\ell \mapsto v}
&&\ts{E-Ref}\\
\estep{\sigma}{\eget{\ell}&}{\sigma[\ell]}{\sigma}
&&\ts{E-Get}\\
\estep{\sigma}{\eset{\ell}{v}&}{()}{\sigma[\ell \mapsto v]}
&&\ts{E-Set}\\
\end{align*}

\subsection{Abbreviations}

We use meta-notation to define type operators, and we
define data types and their constructors via Haskell-like syntax
for GADTs when convenient. 
For instance, we might declare the $\mf{List}$ type as follows:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Haskell,mathescape]
data List a where
  Nil :: List a
  Cons :: a -> List a -> List a
\end{lstlisting}
which desugars to the following type:
$$\mf{List}\ A = \mu \alpha. 1 + (A \times \alpha)$$
and the following constructors:
\begin{align*}
\mf{Nil} &= \einl{()}\\
\mf{Cons} &= \lambda hd.\; \lambda tl.\; \einr{\pair{hd}{tl}}
\end{align*}

(We also say $[A]$ to mean $\mf{List}\ A$.)

Other abbreviations and definition relevant to our translation are as follows:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Haskell,mathescape]
data Attr where
  Attr :: Str -> Str -> Attr
  OnClick :: (1 -> 1) -> Attr
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Haskell,mathescape]
data View where
  Text :: Str -> View
  Tag :: [Attr] -> [View] -> View
  Spec :: $\forall$ a. (a -> (Ref Bool * 1 -> View) * a) -> View
\end{lstlisting}
(Note that the LHS of the $\mf{Spec}$ constructor is \emph{existential}.)

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Haskell,mathescape]
data Tree where
  Lit :: Str -> View
  Node :: ID -> [Attr] -> [View] -> View
  Path :: $\forall$ a. (a -> (Ref Bool * 1 -> View) * a)
    -> (1 -> View) -> (Ref Tree) -> (Ref Bool) -> Tree
\end{lstlisting}

\section{Translation}

From React-tRace Core to $\lambda_\mf{tgt}$.

\subsection{Types}

\begin{align*}
\llrr{\tone} &= \tone\\
\llrr{\tstr} &= \tstr\\
\llrr{A_1 \arrow{E} A_2} &= \llrr{A_1} \rightarrow \llrr{A_2}\\
\llrr{\mf{List}\ A} &= \mf{List}\ \llrr{A}\\
\llrr{\tview} &= \tview\\
\llrr{\tattr} &= \tattr\\
\llrr{\tcomp{A}} &= \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tview\\
\llrr{\tset{A}} &= \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
\end{align*}

\subsection{Values}
\begin{align*}
\llrr{x} &= x\\
\llrr{()} &= ()\\
\llrr{s} &= s\\
\llrr{\lambda x.e} &= \lambda x.\llrr{e}\\
\llrr{[]} &= []\\
\llrr{v :: v} &= \llrr{v} :: \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\C} &= \lambda x.\mf{Spec}(\pair{\C}{x})\\
\llrr{\vtxt{v}} &= \mf{Text}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\vtag{v_1, v_2}} &= \mf{Tag}\ \llrr{v_1}\ \llrr{v_2}\\
\llrr{\vattr{v_1, v_2}} &= \mf{Attr}\ \llrr{v_1}\ \llrr{v_2}\\ 
\llrr{\vclick{v}} &= \mf{OnClick}\ \llrr{v}\\
\end{align*}

\subsection{Expressions}
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\eret{v}} &= \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}} &= \elet{x}{\llrr{e_1}}{\llrr{e_2}}\\
\llrr{v_1\ v_2} &= \llrr{v_1}\ \llrr{v_2}\\
\llrr{\ecase{v}{[]}{e_1&}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}}\\
= \ecase{\llrr{v}}{\einl{\_}}{\llrr{e_1}}{&\einr{\pair{x_\mf{hd}}{x_\mf{tl}}}}{\llrr{e_2}}\\
\end{align*}

\subsection{Bodies}

We introduce two translation functions:
$\llrr{\hat{e}}_\Xi$ and $\llrr{e}^*_\Xi$,
where $\Xi ::= \emptyset \mid \Xi, \pair{x}{\ell}$.
(The former \emph{builds} $\Xi$ and the latter \emph{unrolls} it.)
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}_\Xi &= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}_\Xi}\\
\llrr{e}_\Xi &= \pair{\mf{flag}}{\lambda ().\llrr{e}^*_\Xi}\\
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}} &= \elet{\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}}}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\lambda y.(\eset{\ell}{y} \semi \eset{\mf{flag}}{\mf{true}})}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x}{\eget{\ell}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^*_{\Xi,\pair{x}{\ell}}}
  }
}
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\llrr{e}^*_\emptyset &= \llrr{e}\\
\llrr{e}^*_{\Xi,\pair{x}{\ell}} &= \elet{x}{\eget{\ell}}{\llrr{e}^*_\Xi}
\end{align*}

\subsection{Programs}

\begin{align*}
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{P}} &= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{P}}\\
\llrr{\elet{\C(x)}{\hat{e}}{P}} &=
\elet{\C}{\lambda x. (\elet{\mf{flag}}{\eref{\mf{false}}}
{\llrr{\hat{e}}_\emptyset})}{\llrr{P}}
\end{align*}

% by \ts{E-Let}, we can conclude that 
% $\sigma \semi \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2} \mapsto \elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2} \semi \sigma'$.

% Thus, in either subcase, $\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}$ steps given a proper $\sigma$,
% so the case holds.

% \tb{Case} \ts{TExp-App}:
% We have $\cdot \semi \Sigma \vdashe v_1\ v_2: \ptp_2$.

% \tb{Case} \ts{TExp-If}:
% We have $\cdot \semi \Sigma \vdashe \eif{v}{e_1}{e_2} : \ptp$.

% If $v=true$, then \ts{E-IfTrue} says
